2025-10-04 09:57:43.037 +08:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (24ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `t`.`status` = 'Present' THEN 1
END) AS `present`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `t`.`status` = 'Absent' THEN 1
END) AS `absent`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `t`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN 1
END) AS `underTime`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `t`.`status` = 'Late' THEN 1
END) AS `late`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `t`.`status` = 'On Leave' THEN 1
END) AS `onLeave`
FROM (
    SELECT `h`.`status`, 1 AS `Key`
    FROM `hr_daily_attendance` AS `h`
    INNER JOIN `hr_leave` AS `h0` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `h0`.`employee_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(day FROM `h`.`attendance_date`) = 31) AND (EXTRACT(month FROM `h`.`attendance_date`) = 8)) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `h`.`attendance_date`) = 2025)
) AS `t`
GROUP BY `t`.`Key`

        INNER JOIN `insurance_provider` AS `i21` ON `i20`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i21`.`insurance_provider_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_request` AS `i22` ON `i19`.`patient_id` = `i22`.`patient_id`
        WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `i19`.`date_transact`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `i19`.`date_transact`) = @__year_1)) AND (`i`.`log_id` = `i19`.`log_id`)
        LIMIT 1), 0) AS `c3`, (
        SELECT `i26`.`notes`
        FROM `insurance_logs` AS `i23`
        INNER JOIN `patientinfo` AS `p5` ON `i23`.`patient_id` = `p5`.`patient_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i24` ON `p5`.`patient_id` = `i24`.`patient_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_provider` AS `i25` ON `i24`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i25`.`insurance_provider_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_request` AS `i26` ON `i23`.`patient_id` = `i26`.`patient_id`
        WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `i23`.`date_transact`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `i23`.`date_transact`) = @__year_1)) AND (`i`.`log_id` = `i23`.`log_id`)
        LIMIT 1) AS `c4`, COALESCE((
        SELECT `i27`.`date_transact`
        FROM `insurance_logs` AS `i27`
        INNER JOIN `patientinfo` AS `p6` ON `i27`.`patient_id` = `p6`.`patient_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i28` ON `p6`.`patient_id` = `i28`.`patient_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_provider` AS `i29` ON `i28`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i29`.`insurance_provider_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_request` AS `i30` ON `i27`.`patient_id` = `i30`.`patient_id`
        WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `i27`.`date_transact`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `i27`.`date_transact`) = @__year_1)) AND (`i`.`log_id` = `i27`.`log_id`)
        LIMIT 1), DATE '0001-01-01') AS `c5`, (
        SELECT `i31`.`status`
        FROM `insurance_logs` AS `i31`
        INNER JOIN `patientinfo` AS `p7` ON `i31`.`patient_id` = `p7`.`patient_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i32` ON `p7`.`patient_id` = `i32`.`patient_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_provider` AS `i33` ON `i32`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i33`.`insurance_provider_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_request` AS `i34` ON `i31`.`patient_id` = `i34`.`patient_id`
        WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `i31`.`date_transact`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `i31`.`date_transact`) = @__year_1)) AND (`i`.`log_id` = `i31`.`log_id`)
        LIMIT 1) AS `c6`, COALESCE((
        SELECT `i38`.`insurance_covered`
        FROM `insurance_logs` AS `i35`
        INNER JOIN `patientinfo` AS `p8` ON `i35`.`patient_id` = `p8`.`patient_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i36` ON `p8`.`patient_id` = `i36`.`patient_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_provider` AS `i37` ON `i36`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i37`.`insurance_provider_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_request` AS `i38` ON `i35`.`patient_id` = `i38`.`patient_id`
        WHERE (((EXTRACT(month FROM `i35`.`date_transact`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `i35`.`date_transact`) = @__year_1)) AND (`i`.`log_id` = `i35`.`log_id`)) AND (`i35`.`status` = 'Approved')
        LIMIT 1), 0.0) AS `c7`, COALESCE((
        SELECT `i40`.`claim_amount`
        FROM `insurance_logs` AS `i39`
        INNER JOIN `patientinfo` AS `p9` ON `i39`.`patient_id` = `p9`.`patient_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i40` ON `p9`.`patient_id` = `i40`.`patient_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_provider` AS `i41` ON `i40`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i41`.`insurance_provider_id`
        INNER JOIN `insurance_request` AS `i42` ON `i39`.`patient_id` = `i42`.`patient_id`
        WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `i39`.`date_transact`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `i39`.`date_transact`) = @__year_1)) AND (`i`.`log_id` = `i39`.`log_id`)
        LIMIT 1), 0.0) AS `c8`
    FROM `insurance_logs` AS `i`
    INNER JOIN `patientinfo` AS `p` ON `i`.`patient_id` = `p`.`patient_id`
    INNER JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `p`.`patient_id` = `i0`.`patient_id`
    INNER JOIN `insurance_provider` AS `i1` ON `i0`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i1`.`insurance_provider_id`
    INNER JOIN `insurance_request` AS `i2` ON `i`.`patient_id` = `i2`.`patient_id`
    WHERE (EXTRACT(month FROM `i`.`date_transact`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `i`.`date_transact`) = @__year_1)
    GROUP BY `i`.`log_id`
) AS `t`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT (`i46`.`insurance_covered` / `i44`.`claim_amount`) * 100.0 AS `c`, `i43`.`log_id`, `p10`.`patient_id`, `i44`.`insurance_claims_id`, `i45`.`insurance_provider_id`, `i46`.`request_id`
    FROM `insurance_logs` AS `i43`
    INNER JOIN `patientinfo` AS `p10` ON `i43`.`patient_id` = `p10`.`patient_id`
    INNER JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i44` ON `p10`.`patient_id` = `i44`.`patient_id`
    INNER JOIN `insurance_provider` AS `i45` ON `i44`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i45`.`insurance_provider_id`
    INNER JOIN `insurance_request` AS `i46` ON `i43`.`patient_id` = `i46`.`patient_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `i43`.`date_transact`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `i43`.`date_transact`) = @__year_1)) AND (`i43`.`status` = 'Approved')
) AS `t0` ON `t`.`log_id` = `t0`.`log_id`
ORDER BY `t`.`log_id`, `t0`.`log_id`, `t0`.`patient_id`, `t0`.`insurance_claims_id`, `t0`.`insurance_provider_id`
